			limit = {
				or = {
					has_star_flag = Orc_gos
					has_star_flag = Orc_gos_station
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
				    has_star_flag = Orc_gos
			    }
				create_starbase = {
					owner = ROOT
					size = starbase_outpost
				}
			}
			else = {
				limit = {
					has_star_flag = Orc_gos_station
			    }
				create_starbase = {
					owner = ROOT
					size = starbase_starfortress
					module = shipyard
					module = shipyard
					module = gun_battery
					module = missile_battery
					module = hangar_bay
					module = anchorage 
					
					building = crew_quarters
					building = target_uplink_computer
					building = hydroponics_bay
				}
			}
			every_system_planet = { 
			    if = {                                         ### ПОСТРОЙКА СТАНЦИЙ ###
				    limit = { 
			            is_colonizable = no		
			        }
				    create_mining_station = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
					create_research_station = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
				}
				if = {
				    limit = { 
			            is_colonizable = yes
                        has_planet_flag = Orc_world			
			        }
				    create_colony = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_hive
						}
					}
				    add_building = building_hive_capital 
				    add_building = building_spawning_pool
				    add_building = building_toxic_bath_hive 
				    add_building = building_hive_warren                #building_hive_node
				    add_building = building_sentinel_posts
				    add_building = building_fortress
				    add_building = building_food_processing_center	
				    add_building = building_chemical_plant					
				    while = {
				    	count = 65                            #было 73, 30 03 2024 было 67
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}
					while = {
						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_industrial
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = 4
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_generator
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = 4
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_mining
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = 7
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_farming
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

				}

			        }
				    create_mining_station = {

				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
				}

				    limit = { 
			            is_colonizable = yes
                        has_planet_flag = Agdagat_world			
			        }
				    create_colony = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }

						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_nexus }
					}

						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
					while = {
				    	count = 59
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}
				    add_building = building_machine_major_capital          # building_machine_system_capital
				    add_building = building_machine_assembly_complex  	
				    add_building = building_fortress
					add_building = building_fortress
				    add_building = building_planetary_shield_generator	
				    add_building = building_uplink_node
				    add_building = building_research_lab_2			
				    add_building = building_foundry_2
				    add_building = building_archaeo_refinery	
				    add_building = building_sentinel_posts
					add_building = building_energy_nexus
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_capital = yes
					}
					remove_all_districts = yes
					remove_all_buildings = yes
					while = {
						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_nexus }
					}
					while = {
						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_generator
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
					while = {
				    	count = 61
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}
					add_building = building_machine_major_capital          # building_machine_system_capital
				    add_building = building_machine_assembly_complex  	
				    add_building = building_fortress
					add_building = building_fortress
				    add_building = building_planetary_shield_generator	
				    add_building = building_uplink_node

						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_capital = yes
					}
					remove_all_districts = yes
					remove_all_buildings = yes
					while = {
						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_nexus }
					}
					while = {
						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}

						count = 5

						}
					}

						count = 5

					}

				}
				
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_capital_system = no } #после добавления инициализатора столицы изменить
				create_starbase = {
				owner = ROOT
				size = starbase_outpost
				}
			}
			every_system_planet = { 
			    if = {                                         ### ПОСТРОЙКА СТАНЦИЙ ###
				    limit = { 
			            is_colonizable = no		
			        }
				    create_mining_station = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
					create_research_station = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
				}
				if = {
				    limit = { 
			            is_colonizable = yes
                        has_planet_flag = Nigilah_world
						is_capital = no
			        }
				    create_colony = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
					while = {
						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_nexus }
					}
					while = {
				    	count = 59
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}
				    add_building = building_machine_major_capital          # building_machine_system_capital
				    add_building = building_machine_assembly_complex  	
				    add_building = building_fortress
					add_building = building_fortress
				    add_building = building_planetary_shield_generator	
				    add_building = building_uplink_node
				    add_building = building_research_lab_2			
				    add_building = building_foundry_2
				    add_building = building_archaeo_refinery	
				    add_building = building_sentinel_posts
					add_building = building_energy_nexus
					while = {
						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_capital = yes
					}
					remove_all_districts = yes
					remove_all_buildings = yes
					while = {
						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_nexus }
					}
					while = {
						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_generator
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
					while = {
				    	count = 61
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}
					add_building = building_machine_major_capital          # building_machine_system_capital
				    add_building = building_machine_assembly_complex  	
				    add_building = building_fortress
					add_building = building_fortress

                        has_planet_flag = Kardenat_world

				    	create_pop = {

				    	}

				    add_building = building_archaeo_refinery	
				    add_building = building_sentinel_posts
					add_building = building_energy_nexus

						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
				}

					limit = {
						is_capital = yes
					}

					remove_all_buildings = yes
					while = {
						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_nexus }
					}
					while = {
						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_generator
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
					while = {
				    	count = 61
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}

					}

					}

				    	}
					}

				limit = { is_capital_system = no } #после добавления инициализатора столицы изменить
				create_starbase = {
				owner = ROOT
				size = starbase_outpost
				}
			}
			every_system_planet = { 
			    if = {                                         ### ПОСТРОЙКА СТАНЦИЙ ###
				    limit = { 
			            is_colonizable = no		
			        }
				    create_mining_station = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
					create_research_station = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
				}
				if = {
				    limit = { 
			            is_colonizable = yes
                        has_planet_flag = Nephrekh_world
						is_capital = no
			        }
				    create_colony = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
					while = {
						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_nexus
						}
					}
					while = {
				    	count = 36
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}
				    add_building = building_machine_major_capital          # building_machine_system_capital
				    add_building = building_machine_assembly_complex  	
				    add_building = building_fortress
					add_building = building_fortress
				    add_building = building_planetary_shield_generator	
				    add_building = building_uplink_node
				    add_building = building_research_lab_2			
				    add_building = building_foundry_2
				    add_building = building_archaeo_refinery	
				    add_building = building_sentinel_posts
					add_building = building_energy_nexus
					while = {
						count = 1
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_capital = yes
					}
					remove_all_districts = yes
					remove_all_buildings = yes
					while = {
						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_nexus }
					}
					while = {
						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_generator
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
					while = {
				    	count = 36
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}
					add_building = building_machine_major_capital          # building_machine_system_capital
				    add_building = building_machine_assembly_complex  	
				    add_building = building_fortress
					add_building = building_fortress
				    add_building = building_planetary_shield_generator

					has_star_flag = Sarkoni_gos

				}
				
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_capital_system = no } #после добавления инициализатора столицы изменить
				create_starbase = {
				owner = ROOT
				size = starbase_outpost
				}
			}
			every_system_planet = { 
			    if = {                                         ### ПОСТРОЙКА СТАНЦИЙ ###
				    limit = { 
			            is_colonizable = no		
			        }
				    create_mining_station = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
					create_research_station = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }
				}
				if = {
				    limit = { 
			            is_colonizable = yes
                        has_planet_flag = Sarkoni_world
						is_capital = no
			        }
				    create_colony = {
				    	owner = ROOT
		    	    }

						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_nexus }
					}
					while = {
				    	count = 56
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}
				    add_building = building_machine_major_capital          # building_machine_system_capital
				    add_building = building_machine_assembly_complex  	
				    add_building = building_fortress
					add_building = building_fortress

				    add_building = building_uplink_node
				    add_building = building_research_lab_2			
				    add_building = building_foundry_2
				    add_building = building_archaeo_refinery	
				    add_building = building_sentinel_posts
					add_building = building_energy_nexus
				    while = {
						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_generator }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						is_capital = yes
					}
					remove_all_districts = yes
					remove_all_buildings = yes
					while = {
						count = 8
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_nexus }
					}
					while = {
						count = 3
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_industrial }
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = {
							district = district_generator
						}
					}
					while = {
						count = 5
						add_district_and_planet_size_if_needed_effect = { district = district_mining }
					}
					while = {
				    	count = 61
				    	create_pop = {
				    		species = owner_main_species
				    	}
					}
					add_building = building_machine_major_capital          # building_machine_system_capital
				    add_building = building_machine_assembly_complex  	
				    add_building = building_fortress
					add_building = building_fortress
				    add_building = building_planetary_shield_generator	
				    add_building = building_uplink_node
				    add_building = building_research_lab_2			
				    add_building = building_foundry_2
				    add_building = building_archaeo_refinery	
				    add_building = building_sentinel_posts
					add_building = building_energy_nexus
				}
			}
		}
	}
